sort ListListPiece;
     ListPiece;
     Piece = struct Red | Yellow | None;
     Row = List(Piece);
     Board = List(Row);

cons c_,c_1: ListListPiece;
     c_2,c_3: ListPiece;

map  N,M,R: Pos;
     At: Nat # Nat # Board -> Piece;
     At: Nat # Row -> Piece;
     PutColumn: Piece # Pos # Board -> Board;
     PutColumn,Put: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board -> Board;
     Put: Piece # Pos # Row -> Row;
     Four_in_a_row: Piece # Board # Pos -> Bool;
     Four_in_a_row_horizontally: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board -> Bool;
     Count_rows_horizontally: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board # Nat -> Bool;
     Four_in_a_row_vertically: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board -> Bool;
     Count_rows_vertically: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board # Nat -> Bool;
     Four_in_a_row_diagonally: Piece # Pos # Pos # Board -> Bool;
     Count_rows_diagonally,Count_rows_diagonally': Piece # Pos # Pos # Board # Nat -> Bool;
     initial_board: Board;
     C_ListListPiece: ListListPiece # List(List(Piece)) # List(List(Piece)) -> List(List(Piece));
     Det_ListListPiece: List(List(Piece)) -> ListListPiece;
     C_ListListPiece: ListListPiece # ListListPiece # ListListPiece -> ListListPiece;
     pi_ListListPiece: List(List(Piece)) -> List(Piece);
     pi_ListListPiece1: List(List(Piece)) -> List(List(Piece));
     C_ListListPiece: ListListPiece # List(Piece) # List(Piece) -> List(Piece);
     C_ListPiece: ListPiece # List(Piece) # List(Piece) -> List(Piece);
     Det_ListPiece: List(Piece) -> ListPiece;
     C_ListPiece: ListPiece # ListPiece # ListPiece -> ListPiece;
     pi_ListPiece: List(Piece) -> Piece;
     pi_ListPiece1: List(Piece) -> List(Piece);
     C_ListPiece: ListPiece # Piece # Piece -> Piece;

var  b,b': Board;
     r: Row;
     p,p',p'': Piece;
     x,y: Nat;
     c: Bool;
     z: Pos;
eqn  N  =  7;
     M  =  6;
     R  =  4;
     if(c, true, false)  =  c;
     if(c, false, true)  =  !c;
     if(c, p, p') == p''  =  if(c, p == p'', p' == p'');
     (y == 1)  ->  At(x, y, r |> b)  =  At(x, r);
     (1 < y && y <= M)  ->  At(x, y, r |> b)  =  At(x, Int2Nat(y - 1), b);
     (y == 0 || y > M || x == 0 || x > N)  ->  At(x, y, b)  =  None;
     At(x, y, if(c, b, b'))  =  if(c, At(x, y, b), At(x, y, b'));
     (x == 1)  ->  At(x, p |> r)  =  p;
     (1 < x && x <= N)  ->  At(x, p |> r)  =  At(Int2Nat(x - 1), r);
     (x == 0 || x > N)  ->  At(x, p |> r)  =  None;
     At(x, Put(p, z, r))  =  if(x == z, p, At(x, r));

var  b,b': Board;
     r: Row;
     p,p': Piece;
     x,y,n: Pos;
     dx,dy: Int;
     c,othercolorseen,d4: Bool;
     count_: Nat;
     d1,d2: List(List(Piece));
     d,d5,d6: ListListPiece;
     d3,d7: List(Piece);
eqn  (y == 1)  ->  Put(p, x, y, r |> b)  =  Put(p, x, r) |> b;
     (y > 1 && y <= M)  ->  Put(p, x, y, r |> b)  =  r |> Put(p, x, Int2Pos(y - 1), b);
     Put(p, x, y, if(c, b, b'))  =  if(c, Put(p, x, y, b), Put(p, x, y, b'));
     (x == 1)  ->  Put(p, x, p' |> r)  =  p |> r;
     (x > 1 && x <= N)  ->  Put(p, x, p' |> r)  =  p' |> Put(p, Int2Pos(x - 1), r);
     PutColumn(p, x, b)  =  PutColumn(p, x, 1, b);
     (y < M)  ->  PutColumn(p, x, y, b)  =  if(At(x, y, b) == None, Put(p, x, y, b), PutColumn(p, x, y + 1, b));
     (y == M)  ->  PutColumn(p, x, y, b)  =  Put(p, x, M, b);
     Four_in_a_row_horizontally(p, x, y, b)  =  Count_rows_horizontally(p, x, y, b, 0);
     (x < N)  ->  Count_rows_horizontally(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && (count_ >= R - 1 || Count_rows_horizontally(p, x + 1, y, b, count_ + 1));
     (x == N)  ->  Count_rows_horizontally(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(N, y, b) == p && count_ >= R - 1;
     Four_in_a_row_vertically(p, x, y, b)  =  Count_rows_vertically(p, x, y, b, 0);
     (y < M)  ->  Count_rows_vertically(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && (count_ >= R - 1 || Count_rows_vertically(p, x, y + 1, b, count_ + 1));
     (y == M)  ->  Count_rows_vertically(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, M, b) == p && count_ >= R - 1;
     Four_in_a_row_diagonally(p, x, y, b)  =  Count_rows_diagonally(p, x, y, b, 0) || Count_rows_diagonally'(p, x, y, b, 0);
     (x < N && y < M)  ->  Count_rows_diagonally(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && (count_ >= R - 1 || Count_rows_diagonally(p, x + 1, y + 1, b, count_ + 1));
     (x == N || y == M)  ->  Count_rows_diagonally(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && count_ >= R - 1;
     (x < N && y > 1)  ->  Count_rows_diagonally'(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && (count_ >= R - 1 || Count_rows_diagonally'(p, x + 1, max(y - 1, 1), b, count_ + 1));
     (x == N || y == 1)  ->  Count_rows_diagonally'(p, x, y, b, count_)  =  At(x, y, b) == p && count_ >= R - 1;
     initial_board  =  [[None, None, None, None, None, None, None], [None, None, None, None, None, None, None], [None, None, None, None, None, None, None], [None, None, None, None, None, None, None], [None, None, None, None, None, None, None], [None, None, None, None, None, None, None]];
     C_ListListPiece(c_, d1, d2)  =  d1;
     C_ListListPiece(c_1, d1, d2)  =  d2;
     C_ListListPiece(d, d2, d2)  =  d2;
     Det_ListListPiece([])  =  c_;
     Det_ListListPiece(d3 |> d1)  =  c_1;
     Det_ListListPiece(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, Det_ListListPiece(d1), Det_ListListPiece(d2));
     C_ListListPiece(c_, d5, d6)  =  d5;
     C_ListListPiece(c_1, d5, d6)  =  d6;
     C_ListListPiece(d, d6, d6)  =  d6;
     C_ListListPiece(d, c_, c_1)  =  d;
     Det_ListListPiece(C_ListListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListListPiece(d, Det_ListListPiece(d1), Det_ListListPiece(d2));
     pi_ListListPiece(d3 |> d1)  =  d3;
     pi_ListListPiece([])  =  [];
     pi_ListListPiece(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, pi_ListListPiece(d1), pi_ListListPiece(d2));
     C_ListListPiece(c_, d3, d7)  =  d3;
     C_ListListPiece(c_1, d3, d7)  =  d7;
     C_ListListPiece(d, d7, d7)  =  d7;
     pi_ListListPiece(C_ListListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListListPiece(d, pi_ListListPiece(d1), pi_ListListPiece(d2));
     pi_ListListPiece1(d3 |> d1)  =  d1;
     pi_ListListPiece1([])  =  [];
     pi_ListListPiece1(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, pi_ListListPiece1(d1), pi_ListListPiece1(d2));
     pi_ListListPiece1(C_ListListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListListPiece(d, pi_ListListPiece1(d1), pi_ListListPiece1(d2));

var  d1,d2: List(Piece);
     d,d5,d6: ListPiece;
     d3,d7: Piece;
     d4: Bool;
eqn  C_ListPiece(c_2, d1, d2)  =  d1;
     C_ListPiece(c_3, d1, d2)  =  d2;
     C_ListPiece(d, d2, d2)  =  d2;
     Det_ListPiece([])  =  c_2;
     Det_ListPiece(d3 |> d1)  =  c_3;
     Det_ListPiece(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, Det_ListPiece(d1), Det_ListPiece(d2));
     C_ListPiece(c_2, d5, d6)  =  d5;
     C_ListPiece(c_3, d5, d6)  =  d6;
     C_ListPiece(d, d6, d6)  =  d6;
     C_ListPiece(d, c_2, c_3)  =  d;
     Det_ListPiece(C_ListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListPiece(d, Det_ListPiece(d1), Det_ListPiece(d2));
     pi_ListPiece(d3 |> d1)  =  d3;
     pi_ListPiece([])  =  None;
     pi_ListPiece(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, pi_ListPiece(d1), pi_ListPiece(d2));
     C_ListPiece(c_2, d3, d7)  =  d3;
     C_ListPiece(c_3, d3, d7)  =  d7;
     C_ListPiece(d, d7, d7)  =  d7;
     pi_ListPiece(C_ListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListPiece(d, pi_ListPiece(d1), pi_ListPiece(d2));
     pi_ListPiece1(d3 |> d1)  =  d1;
     pi_ListPiece1([])  =  [];
     pi_ListPiece1(if(d4, d1, d2))  =  if(d4, pi_ListPiece1(d1), pi_ListPiece1(d2));
     pi_ListPiece1(C_ListPiece(d, d1, d2))  =  C_ListPiece(d, pi_ListPiece1(d1), pi_ListPiece1(d2));

act  YellowMoves,RedMoves,YellowWins,RedWins;
